We release a document-level gold LID set whose guideline keeps integrated borrowings as Kazakh and reserves mixed for clause-level switches, plus a mixed-only sentiment pool used after LID in a filter-first cascade. On a shared LID test, FastText, Lingua, raw and windowed HeLI, character-trigram NB, and XLM-R range from weak to strong performance. The gap shows the bottleneck is the loanword-vs-switch annotation boundary, not model class alone.

</details>


### [91] [Writing-System-Level Tokenizer Adaptation for Byte-Level BPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00582)
*Bohdan Didenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在保持模型词汇量不变的情况下，如何高效地对低资源语言进行字节级BPE分词器的后处理适应。针对直接迁移语言特定分词器时可能出现的合并顺序冲突问题，提出BPE引导插入方法，确保新词元可通过目标合并图可达。该方法结合脚本感知行选择、目标脚本字节级前驱重建和引导插入，显著降低乌克兰语的分词数量（分别减少33.5%和36.6%），同时几乎不改变英语及四个欧洲语言的平均分词表现，并保留约78.5%/77.3%的原始词元ID分配。与其他方法相比，该方法在压缩效果与兼容性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练字节级BPE分词器对低资源语言分割效率低下。直接更换分词器会改变几乎所有词元ID，而扩展词汇表则增加模型嵌入和输出矩阵大小。因此需要一种无需修改模型结构、保持词元ID兼容性的后处理适应方法。

Method: 提出BPE引导插入机制，通过目标可到达分解构建转移词元；结合脚本感知行选择减少碎片化，重建目标脚本的字节级前提条件，并保证合并图可达性；采用约束匹配的全局与频率移除策略优化压缩效果。

Result: 在乌克兰语上的应用中，分词数减少33.5%至36.6%，英语及四语言欧洲集合的分词变化小于0.05%，保留约78.5%/77.3%的原词元ID；其他方法如重训练虽压缩略优但丧失大部分相同ID词元，且提升英语分词量7.6-8.6%；三语言西里尔字母小集合分词量略有上升（6.7%/10.1%）；结构审计确认所有新增节点均可通过正常排序合并达到，无原有同ID词元被破坏。

Conclusion: BPE引导插入提供了一种高效且兼容的后处理适应方案，在保持模型结构不变的前提下显著改善低资源语言分词性能，兼具高压缩率与强兼容性，适用于多语言模型的轻量级本地化部署。

Abstract: Pretrained byte-level BPE tokenizers can segment underrepresented languages inefficiently. Replacing a tokenizer changes the meaning of nearly every token ID, while vocabulary expansion enlarges the model's embedding and output matrices. We study post-hoc adaptation that keeps the model-vocabulary size fixed and preserves most existing token-to-ID assignments as a construction-time compatibility property. Directly transferring tokens from a language-specific tokenizer does not guarantee derivability through the target BPE merge graph: an inserted entry can conflict with the target's greedy merge ranks. We formalize this failure as the merge ordering problem and introduce BPE-guided insertion, which builds each transferred token through a target-reachable decomposition. Our pipeline uses script-aware row selection to limit collateral fragmentation, reconstructs target-script byte-level prerequisites, and applies guided insertion to maintain merge-graph reachability. On Ukrainian adaptations of Nemotron and GPT-OSS, it reduces token counts by 33.5% and 36.6%, keeps changes on English and the evaluated four-language European aggregate within 0.05%, and retains 78.5%/77.3% of original model-vocabulary rows at the same IDs. Constraint-matched global and frequency-based removal achieve similar Ukrainian compression but increase English/European token counts by 0.7-2.2%; fresh same-size retraining compresses Ukrainian slightly more but retains effectively no same-ID rows and increases English token counts by 7.6-8.6%. The reallocation increases token counts on the evaluated three-language Cyrillic micro-aggregate by 6.7%/10.1%. Structural audits find all 28,134/45,398 inserted BPE nodes reachable under ordinary rank-ordered merging and no retained same-ID model-vocabulary entry newly broken. We release all tokenizers and code.

</details>


### [92] [Verification Without Sufficiency: Per-Chunk Filtering Fails on Multi-Hop RAG, and Decomposition Repairs It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00585)
*Randhir Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成中基于片段评分的验证方法在多跳问题上的失效原因，并提出改进方案。传统方法假设每个检索片段独立足以支持答案，但多跳问题要求多个片段协同推理，且答案所在段落往往不在原始问题中提及。实验表明，基于蕴含关系的片段评分在多跳数据集上表现显著低于单跳任务（如SQuAD），而通过将验证条件从原问题改为分解后的子问题，可大幅提升后续推理阶段的蕴含得分（如MuSiQue上从0.546升至0.840）。即使使用现成的Qwen2.5-7B分解器，也能达到0.637，捕获大部分性能上限。同时发现，当前迭代检索系统虽生成分解结果却在验证前丢弃，导致信息浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成中的每片段评分机制无法有效处理多跳问答任务，因其假设单个片段可独立支撑答案，而多跳问题的本质是依赖多个非直接提及的片段进行联合推理，因此需要新的验证范式以提升准确性。

Method: 提出将验证过程从基于原问题转向基于分解后的子问题，利用子问题引导对检索片段的语义蕴含判断；结合真实分解标签与现成语言模型（Qwen2.5-7B）进行自动分解，评估不同设置下的验证效果。

Result: 在三个多跳数据集（HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA、MuSiQue）上，传统每片段过滤方法显著劣于不过滤，且随着生成器能力增强，差距扩大；采用子问题分解后，蕴含得分大幅提升（如MuSiQue上+0.355），且现成分解器已能实现可观性能（0.637），证明该策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于原始问题的片段评分机制不适合多跳问答；必须将验证条件迁移至子问题层面，才能有效捕捉跨片段推理逻辑。现有系统若保留中间分解结构，可显著提升整体性能。

Abstract: Verification for retrieval-augmented generation usually scores each retrieved chunk and drops the ones that fail. We show this cannot work for multi-hop questions, and show what does. Per-chunk scoring assumes one chunk is a sufficient premise for the answer. Multi-hop questions are built so that none is, and the paragraph carrying the answer is the one the question does not name. Entailment scoring reaches 0.643, 0.523 and 0.560 AUC on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA and MuSiQue, against 0.951 on single-hop SQuAD. Seven controls rule out model capacity, premise length, hypothesis template, decision threshold, retriever, answer-matching criterion and prompt. End to end across three datasets, three generator sizes and two prompts, per-chunk gating is significantly worse than not filtering at all in every cell, and its penalty grows with generator capability. The repair is to condition verification on the decomposed sub-question rather than the original query. Using MuSiQue's gold decomposition, entailment on a later hop rises from 0.546, which is chance, to 0.840, a paired lift of +0.355 with a bootstrap interval of [0.331, 0.382]. An off-the-shelf Qwen2.5-7B decomposer, given the question and the top retrieved paragraph, reaches 0.637 and captures 31% of that ceiling; decomposing without retrieval reaches 0.533, below the original question. Iterative retrieval systems already produce such decompositions and discard them before verifying.

</details>


### [93] [A Heuristic Perspective on Debiasing Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00622)
*Tian Lan,Yemin Wang,Chuancheng Shi,Xiangyu Wu,Zesheng Shi,Yuan Wang,Jiang Li,Guanglai Gao,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HEIMAT 是一种启发式自动去偏框架，通过两步法（偏见披露和去偏微调）有效减少语言模型中的偏见，无需人工标注且计算成本低，同时保持自然语言理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去偏方法依赖反事实增强或表示投影，存在计算成本高、难以扩展到大模型以及需要人工标注数据的问题，限制了其在多文化场景下的应用。

Method: HEIMAT 采用两个步骤：1）使用简单模板构造启发式提示以揭示模型偏见并生成上下文提示；2）通过最小化这些上下文提示上的预测的 Jensen-Shannon 散度来微调模型，从而降低偏见。

Result: 实验表明，HEIMAT 在不同文化背景下均能有效减轻偏见，同时保持模型的自然语言理解能力。

Conclusion: HEIMAT 提供了一种高效、可扩展且无需人工标注的自动去偏方案，适用于大规模语言模型在多文化环境中的公平性优化。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) often acquire various biases during pre-training and may express them in interactions, potentially causing social harm. Existing methods often rely on counterfactual augmentation or representation projection. These strategies remain limited in practice due to their high computational costs and difficulty in scaling to larger models. Additionally, many of these strategies require manual data annotation, narrowing their scope to specific cultures and bias categories. To overcome these limitations, we propose HEIMAT, a HEurIstic-style autoMATic debiasing framework for LMs. HEIMAT consists of two main steps: bias disclosure and debiasing fine-tuning. In the first step, it uses simple templates to construct heuristic prompts, which are applied to reveal model biases and generate corresponding context prompts. In the second step, it fine-tunes the model by minimizing the Jensen-Shannon divergence of predictions on these context prompts to reduce bias. Extensive experiments show that HEIMAT effectively mitigates bias in different cultures while maintaining the model's natural language understanding (NLU) performance.

</details>


### [94] [TreeProbe : A Tibetan Medicine Benchmark for Cultural Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00640)
*Jin Zhang,Linyu Li,Weili Jiang,Yuqing Cai,Yutong Liu,Guanquecairang,Yongbin Yu,Jingye Cai,Nyima Tashi,Gadeng Luosang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出TreeProbe，首个基于藏医学本体框架的偏见评估基准，旨在检测大语言模型在藏医学知识体系中的文化偏见。通过4,719个专家评审的问题，覆盖467种疾病和10个子任务，发现当前模型在藏医学语境下表现有限，且存在系统性外部本体漂移现象。模型偏向生物医学或中医推理，受预训练数据构成和中藏医学表面相似性影响。该基准为开发语言包容且认知公平的医疗AI提供诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗应用中常反映主流高资源医学传统，忽视如藏医学等传统医学体系。藏医学具有独立完整的理论体系，但当前模型缺乏对其本质理解，易在推理中扭曲其原生知识结构。然而，缺乏量化评估文化偏见的工具，限制了对模型公平性的检验与改进。

Method: 提出TreeProbe，基于藏医学‘医学之树’本体框架构建，包含4,719个经专家审核的测试项，覆盖467种疾病及10个子任务，涵盖三个根本要素（三根）。采用多模型对比实验，分析模型在藏医学情境下的表现，并探究其本体漂移模式及其成因。

Result: 实验表明，当前主流大语言模型在藏医学语境下表现不佳，普遍存在系统性外部本体漂移；部分模型倾向于向生物医学靠拢，另一些则更接近中医推理，这种差异由预训练数据组成及中藏医学间的表面相似性所塑造。

Conclusion: TreeProbe是首个针对藏医学文化偏见的诊断性基准，有助于推动医疗AI系统实现语言包容与认知公正。未来需结合本土知识体系优化模型训练与评估机制，以促进全球健康公平。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly viewed as a potential means of mitigating global health inequities, yet their outputs often reflect dominant high-resource medical traditions and provide limited coverage of traditional medical knowledge systems. Tibetan medicine, one of the world's four major traditional medical systems, has an independent and highly structured theoretical framework. When models lack grounded understanding of Tibetan medicine, they may fall back on dominant epistemic systems and distort the native knowledge structure during reasoning. However, quantitative tools for evaluating cultural bias in Tibetan medicine remain largely absent. To address this gap, we introduce TreeProbe, the first cultural-bias benchmark organized around the native Tree of Medicine framework in Tibetan medicine. It contains 4,719 expert-adjudicated items covering 467 diseases and 10 subtasks along the three roots. Experiments on representative LLMs show that current models remain limited in native Tibetan medical contexts and exhibit systematic external ontology drift. Further analysis reveals that models diverge in whether they drift toward biomedical or TCM reasoning, shaped by pretraining data composition and surface resemblance between TCM and Tibetan medicine. TreeProbe provides a diagnostic benchmark for developing medical AI systems that are both linguistically inclusive and epistemically fair. Code and data are available in an anonymous repository at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TreeProbe/.

</details>


### [95] [Select-And-Extract: A Lightweight Plugin for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00658)
*Chenming Tang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SANE 是一种轻量级的 RAG 插件，通过增强检索和阅读阶段的表现来解决 RAG 的两大失败模式。它利用语义检索器获取大量候选内容，再由语言模型根据摘要筛选最优项以提升召回率；在阅读阶段，采用蓝图引导的实时证据提取，使生成模型仅处理结构化关键信息，从而提升推理能力。实验表明 SANE 在少量额外开销下显著提升性能，证明高性能 RAG 不必依赖复杂架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法在检索失败和阅读失败方面存在局限：固定结构索引泛化能力差，查询时结构化方法依赖大量语言模型调用且对模型能力要求高。因此需要一种更高效、通用且低开销的解决方案。

Method: SANE 采用两阶段设计：1）检索阶段，先使用语义检索器获取广泛候选，再由语言模型基于摘要选择最优项，提高召回率；2）阅读阶段，通过蓝图引导的查询时证据提取，将信息提炼为紧凑、结构化的关键点，供生成模型使用，增强推理能力。

Result: 实验证明，SANE 在多个基准上显著提升 RAG 性能，同时仅引入适度的额外计算开销。其效果优于一些复杂的替代方法，展现出良好的实用性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: SANE 作为一种轻量级插件，有效缓解了 RAG 中的检索与阅读失败问题，证明高性能 RAG 系统无需过度复杂，简洁设计同样可实现优异表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for language model (LM) systems fundamentally has two failure modes: retrieval failure and reading failure. The former fails to recall the right pieces of information from the external corpus, and the latter fails to produce the correct answer although the right information is retrieved. Some methods perform structured indexing for retrieval failure, but may suffer from limited generalization of the fixed structures. Some methods perform query-time structuring for reading failure, but typically require a lot of LM calls and rely heavily on the LM's capability. To this end, we propose Select-ANd-Extract (SANE), a simple yet effective plugin for RAG. For the retrieval failure, we retrieve a wide set of candidates with a semantic retriever, and leverage the LM to select the top candidates based on their synopses, which yields better recall than the original retriever. For the reading failure, we perform blueprint-guided query-time evidence extraction, which allows the generator LM to use only compact and structured key information so that it can perform better reasoning. Empirical results confirm that SANE brings solid improvements, while only introducing modest extra overhead. As a lightweight plugin for RAG, SANE offers a simple alternative to heavier approaches, and suggests a high-performance RAG framework need not be overly complex.

</details>


### [96] [OpenART: Scaling Agent Red Teaming via Open-Ended Environment Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00677)
*Yunhao Chen,Xin Wang,Yixu Wang,Yi Liu,Jie Li,Yan Teng,Xingjun Ma,Xia Hu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OpenART，一个用于可扩展智能体红队测试的开放环境，通过环境演化发现累积性安全风险。引入EMHA方法，一种黑盒策略，通过反馈驱动的环境演化实现高效攻击，无需参数更新。在75种代理模型配置下，平均攻击成功率达85.0%，复杂环境中优势更显著。结果表明，运行时实现对安全性的贡献远超模型本身能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准无法捕捉智能体在持久环境中因早期状态变化引发的长期累积风险，因聚焦于短时静态任务。需要新的评估框架来研究复杂、演化的环境中的智能体安全性。

Method: 构建OpenART平台，包含超过10,000个验证的状态化场景和50个领域，支持超过50万工具与技能。采用进化马尔可夫超图攻击（EMHA）策略，通过协调授权状态转移实现环境演化，保持任务目标不变，仅改变环境状态。

Result: EMHA在所有配置中实现85.0%的平均攻击成功率；在复杂环境中相比仅靠指令演化的攻击方式提升超过17%。运行时实现对安全表现的影响显著大于模型能力差异。

Conclusion: OpenART为研究复杂、动态环境中的智能体安全提供了可扩展的基础，环境演化是揭示深层安全缺陷的关键手段，运行时设计对安全性有决定性影响。

Abstract: AI agents operate in persistent environments where early state changes can influence decisions far into the future. Unlike conventional language-model interactions, agent behavior is mediated through a shared state that is repeatedly modified and reused across long-horizon workflows. Current safety benchmarks often fail to capture these cumulative risks because they focus on short, static tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce OpenART, an open-ended arena for scalable agent red teaming through environment evolution. OpenART provides over 10,000 validated stateful scenarios across 50 domains, drawing from a pool of more than 500,000 tools and skills. These tasks require a median of 97 tool calls and enable unified evaluation across 75 different agent-model configurations. To systematically explore these evolving attack surfaces, we propose the Evolutionary Markov Hypergraph Attack (EMHA). EMHA is a black-box policy that performs feedback-driven environment evolution by coordinating authorized state transitions without requiring parameter updates. Throughout the evaluation, task objectives remain fixed while only the environment state changes. Across all configurations, EMHA achieves a pooled Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 85.0%. Its advantage over instruction-only evolution increases from approximately 2% on simple environments to over 17% on the most complex ones, demonstrating that environment evolution increasingly exposes safety failures as task complexity grows. Furthermore, our analysis shows that the specific runtime implementation of an agent explains a significant portion of safety variation beyond the underlying model's capabilities. These results establish OpenART as a scalable foundation for studying agent safety in complex, evolving environments.

</details>


### [97] [AttnLink: Turning Attention into Schema Links for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00693)
*Jinwang Song,Tao Liu,Haowen Zheng,Xiangheng Li,Yifan Li,Hongying Zan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AttnLink 是一种基于注意力机制的框架，将大语言模型（LLM）内部注意力转化为连续相关性得分，用于模式链接。它通过单次预填充推理实现所有候选项的排序，无需自回归解码，显著提升效率。提出两个变体：AttnLink-U 直接利用预训练注意力，AttnLink-S 通过监督对齐注意力分布以提升准确率。为增强多相关模式项覆盖，采用集合质量目标与自适应概率下界正则化。得分支持后处理精度-召回率调节（如温度缩放和累积质量选择）。在 Spider、BIRD、Spider2-SQLite 上表现优异，mAP 分别达 99.22%、95.95%、83.29%，延迟仅为毫秒级，并在九组生成器-数据集设置中取得最佳或并列最佳执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到 SQL 系统中的模式链接方法在上下文建模能力、基于分数的可控性和推理效率之间存在权衡。需要一种既能高效处理又能灵活控制且具备强建模能力的方案。

Method: 将 LLM 内部注意力从生成起始位置映射到候选模式跨度，生成连续相关性得分；使用单次预填充完成所有候选排序；设计 AttnLink-U 和 AttnLink-S 两种变体，其中 AttnLink-S 通过监督学习对齐真实模式项；引入集合质量目标与自适应概率下界正则化以提升多匹配覆盖；支持通过温度缩放和累积质量选择进行后处理精度-召回率调节。

Result: 在 Spider、BIRD、Spider2-SQLite 数据集上，AttnLink-S 的 mAP 分别达到 99.22%、95.95%、83.29%，推理延迟在毫秒级；在七种生成器-数据集组合中，下游 SQL 生成任务的执行准确率达到最优或并列最优。

Conclusion: AttnLink 成功实现了高精度、低延迟和可调控的模式链接，在多个基准上表现卓越，为文本到 SQL 系统提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Schema linking is a critical component of Text-to-SQL systems, but existing approaches often trade off contextual modeling capacity, score-based controllability, and inference efficiency. We introduce AttnLink, an attention-based framework that converts LLMs' internal attention into continuous relevance scores for schema items. AttnLink extracts the attention from the generation-start position to candidate schema spans, enabling all candidates to be ranked in a single prefill pass without autoregressive decoding. We develop two variants: AttnLink-U, which directly probes pretrained attention without parameter updates, and AttnLink-S, which aligns the attention distribution with gold schema items through direct supervision. To improve coverage of multiple relevant schema items, AttnLink-S combines a set-mass objective with an adaptive probability-floor regularizer. The resulting scores support post-hoc precision-recall control through temperature scaling and cumulative-mass selection. Experiments on Spider, BIRD, and Spider2-SQLite show that AttnLink-S achieves mAP scores of 99.22%, 95.95%, and 83.29%, respectively, with millisecond-scale schema-linking latency. It also yields the best or tied-best execution accuracy for downstream SQL generation in seven of nine generator-dataset settings.

</details>


### [98] [Exploiting Intrinsic Duality for Multi-Hop Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00712)
*Maodong Li,Xinyue Kang,Yuanchen Shi,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QQ框架通过利用问答任务的双重性，提出一种统一架构同时实现多跳问题生成（MQG）和问答（QA），通过双向对齐约束和对比学习增强问题与答案间的对应关系，显著提升生成问题的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多忽视了多跳问题生成（MQG）与问答（QA）之间的内在对偶性，导致无法充分利用两者之间的相互依赖关系。

Method: 提出QQ框架，采用统一架构同时作为MQG和QA模型，引入双向对齐约束和对比学习机制，强化问题与答案之间的对应关系。

Result: 在HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上的自动与人工评估表明，QQ框架显著提升了多跳问题生成的质量。

Conclusion: QQ框架有效利用了问答任务的对偶性，通过双向对齐和对比学习增强了生成问题的准确性和相关性，为多跳问题生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi hop question generation (MQG) aims to generate questions from multiple given documents and target answers, whereas question answering (QA) focuses on deriving answers from documents given specific questions. Although MQG and QA are inherently dual tasks, most existing MQG studies largely overlook this intrinsic duality. To address this limitation, we propose QQ, a novel framework that exploits the duality between Question and answer for multi hop Question generation. Specifically, QQ employs a unified architecture functioning simultaneously as both an MQG and a QA model to fully leverage their interdependence. Our framework is driven by two key mechanisms: (i) enforcing bidirectional alignment constraints to ensure strict mutual correspondence between the questions generated by the MQG model and the answers produced by the QA model; and (ii) applying contrastive learning to pull paired question answer representations closer while pushing unpaired ones apart, thereby reinforcing this correspondence. Extensive automatic and human evaluations on the HotpotQA and MuSiQue datasets demonstrate that the QQ framework significantly improves the quality of generated multi hop questions.

</details>


### [99] [Observatorio Lazaro: A self-populating database of anglicism usage in the Spanish press](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00713)
*Elena Alvarez-Mellado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Observatorio Lázaro es un recurso lingüístico que monitorea préstamos léxicos no asimilados (principalmente anglicismos) en la prensa digital española desde abril de 2020. Utiliza un modelo neuronal de etiquetado secuencial para detectar estos préstamos automáticamente, procesando diariamente artículos de múltiples medios. El sistema genera una base de datos diacrónica actualizada con más de dos millones de préstamos en 1,88 millones de artículos y 993 millones de tokens (2020–2026). Se evalúa mediante métricas de precisión (F1=0,86), acuerdo entre anotadores (kappa=0,91) y auditoría manual (precisión del 94%). Los resultados indican una frecuencia estable de aproximadamente dos anglicismos por mil tokens, con un vocabulario en crecimiento y mayor presencia en secciones como moda, tecnología y estilo de vida. El recurso está disponible a través de una interfaz web y API, y tiene como objetivo complementar diccionarios estáticos y corpora anotados puntuales.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La necesidad de monitorear dinámicamente el uso de anglicismos no asimilados en el español contemporáneo, especialmente en los medios digitales, para comprender sus patrones de adopción y evolución en tiempo real, superando las limitaciones de recursos estáticos como diccionarios o corpora anotadas de forma puntual.

Method: Se desarrolló un pipeline automatizado que incluye adquisición de textos diarios de medios digitales, detección de préstamos léxicos mediante un modelo de etiquetado secuencial basado en redes neuronales, post-procesamiento, almacenamiento estructurado y acceso público vía API e interfaz web.

Result: El sistema ha recopilado más de dos millones de anglicismos en 1,88 millones de artículos y 993 millones de tokens (2020–2026). La detección alcanza un F1 de 0,86 a nivel de span, el acuerdo inter-annotador fue de kappa=0,91, y la auditoría manual mostró una precisión del 94%. El análisis muestra una tasa constante de aproximadamente dos anglicismos por mil tokens, con un vocabulario abierto y en crecimiento, concentrado principalmente en temas de moda, tecnología y estilo de vida.

Conclusion: Observatorio Lázaro proporciona un registro continuo y dinámico de préstamos léxicos en el español contemporáneo, ofreciendo una herramienta valiosa para la lexicografía, la lingüística del lenguaje en medios y el estudio de neologismos, complementando adecuadamente los recursos estáticos existentes.

Abstract: This paper describes Observatorio Lázaro, a language resource that monitors unassimilated lexical borrowings (predominantly English lexical borrowings or anglicisms) in the Spanish digital press. Since April 2020 the system has automatically processed the daily output of a collection of news outlets, detected borrowings with a neural sequence-labeling model, and made the results available through a public web interface and API. The result is a continuously updated diachronic database which, at the time of writing, records more than two million borrowings across 1.88 million articles and 993 million running tokens of text (2020-2026). The paper documents the resource: we describe the end-to-end pipeline (acquisition, detection, post-processing, storage and access), the data model and the terms of availability; we evaluate the resource through the detector's held-out performance (span-level F1=0.86 for the borrowing class), inter-annotator agreement on the training corpus (Cohen's kappa=0.91) and a manual precision audit of 1,000 spans from the deployed data; and we situate it with respect to Spanish borrowing lexicography, annotated borrowing corpora and neology-monitoring observatories. The data shows that unassimilated anglicisms are used in the Spanish press at a frequency of approximately two anglicisms per thousand tokens, and that this rate remains stable. Our statistical analysis over six years reveals that the anglicism vocabulary in Spanish behaves as an open and growing class, with 58.7% of its types attested only once (53.6% after correcting for detection precision), and that its density is highest in the fashion, technology and lifestyle sections and lowest in political and institutional news. The resource is intended to complement static borrowing dictionaries and one-off annotated corpora by providing a continuously updated record of borrowing in the Spanish press.

</details>


### [100] [RAGOCR: Optical Compression of Retrieval-Augmented Text via Visual Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00765)
*Jiayang Yu,Jialun Zhong,Lei Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGOCR提出一种新的检索增强生成框架，通过查询感知的视觉化压缩方式，在保持高信息保真度的同时显著降低输入令牌数量。其动态分辨率机制根据文档的相关性和复杂性自适应分配视觉粒度，实现高效且精准的上下文压缩。在五个问答基准测试中，RAGOCR在准确率上超越原始RAG超过15%，同时仅需其1/8的输入令牌数，并优于硬压缩和软压缩基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理长篇检索上下文时面临高昂计算成本的问题。硬压缩方法虽能在线查询感知但压缩率低且需微调生成模型；软压缩虽压缩率高却依赖离线编码，不考虑查询内容。因此亟需一种兼顾压缩效率与信息保留、同时支持查询感知的新型压缩方案。

Method: RAGOCR将检索到的文档转化为基于输入查询的紧凑视觉表示，采用查询感知的动态分辨率机制：相关性强的文档以高分辨率渲染以保留细节，边缘文档则以低分辨率大幅压缩，从而实现资源与精度之间的平衡。

Result: 在使用MedOmniKB检索语料库的五个问答基准测试中，RAGOCR相比原始RAG提升准确率超过15%，仅需1/8的输入令牌数，且在不同检索深度下均优于硬压缩与软压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: RAGOCR通过引入查询感知的视觉压缩与动态分辨率机制，有效解决了传统RAG压缩方法中的性能与效率矛盾问题，实现了高压缩比与高信息保真度的统一，为知识密集型问答系统提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become essential for knowledge-intensive question answering, yet scaling RAG pipelines remains challenging due to the prohibitive computational cost of processing lengthy retrieved contexts. Existing compression approaches face a fundamental trade-off: hard compression methods operate online in a query-aware fashion but achieve only modest compression rates and typically require fine-tuning the generative model, while soft compression methods attain higher ratios but rely on costly offline encoding that is entirely agnostic to the input query. To bridge this gap, we introduce RAGOCR, a novel framework that compresses retrieved documents into compact visual representations conditioned on the input query. To further balance compression rate and information fidelity, we introduce a query-aware dynamic resolution mechanism that adaptively allocates visual granularity based on each document's estimated relevance and complexity: highly relevant passages are rendered at higher resolution to preserve fine-grained details, while peripheral documents are aggressively compressed at lower resolution. Experiments on five QA benchmarks using the MedOmniKB retrieval corpus demonstrate that RAGOCR surpasses naive RAG by over 15\% in accuracy while requiring only one-eighth the number of input tokens, and consistently outperforms both hard and soft compression baselines across varying retrieval depths.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过保守-一致编码（CCE）框架重新审视了莱布尼茨的磨坊、图灵的模仿游戏和塞尔的中文房间，提出在符号化设置中，行为表现（$W_{causal,T}$）与内部结构效率（$κ_T$）可分离；尽管查表系统与生成系统可实现相似性能，但后者在$κ_T$上显著更优，因其复用紧凑内部结构，而前者依赖不断扩展的未复用映射。该区分有助于厘清理解的本质，并为未来人工智能安全分析提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视经典哲学思想实验，以解决关于理解与意识的争议，强调行为表现与支撑其内部结构组织之间的区别，为人工智能安全性提供新视角。

Method: 构建一个简化的符号化模型，引入任务表现（$W_{causal,T}$）与操作性意识（$κ_T$）作为评估指标，比较查表系统与生成系统的内部结构效率。

Result: 查表系统与生成系统可在任务表现上相当，但在$κ_T$上存在显著差异；生成系统因复用紧凑结构而表现出更高的内部效率。

Conclusion: 应将外在行为表现与内在结构组织相分离，这一区分对理解智能本质及未来AI安全分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [102] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM 是一个自进化的大语言模型代理，能够仅根据自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行并优化 OpenFOAM 模拟，显著降低 CFD 使用门槛。通过在 Qwen-coder 2.5-14B 基础上微调，并结合 7 个求解器、13 种参数化网格模板和 y+ 感知数值策略，实现高效自动化。其核心为包含 7 个阶段的进化循环，并引入三种抗退化机制（RAG 增强重试上下文、字典级手术式修复、提示多样性改写）以确保模型稳定性。该系统推动了 CFD 快速原型设计与普及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用 OpenFOAM 等开源流体动力学求解器需要大量专业知识和繁琐配置，阻碍了工程应用的普及。亟需一种能理解自然语言指令并自主完成完整仿真流程的智能系统，以降低技术门槛并加速研发。

Method: 基于预训练的 Qwen-coder 2.5-14B 模型，针对 7 种 OpenFOAM 求解器、13 种参数化网格模板及 y+ 自适应数值策略进行微调；构建包含 7 个阶段的自进化循环，结合 RAG 增强重试上下文、字典级修复与提示多样性改写三种机制防止模型退化，实现从自然语言到完整仿真的端到端自动化。

Result: AutoFOAM 能够准确解析自然语言指令，自动生成符合要求的 OpenFOAM 配置文件，成功运行多种复杂流动场景模拟，并在重复训练中保持稳定性能，显著提升仿真效率与可访问性。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM 成功将生成式 AI 与高精度流体模拟融合，实现了 CFD 工作流的自动化与民主化，为工程设计与科研提供了高效、易用的新范式。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [103] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在中小企业（SMEs）中的应用，重点解决其生成内容中的幻觉问题。提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种增强方法，通过引入外部知识源提升生成内容的准确性与可信度。实验在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等主流LLM上进行，评估指标包括响应质量、幻觉风险、上下文相关性及人工可解释性。结果表明，RAG增强的LLMs能显著降低幻觉，提升决策可靠性与上下文感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在中小企业中广泛应用，但其生成内容中的幻觉问题导致信息误导，削弱用户信任。亟需有效方法提升LLMs输出的可靠性与可信度，以支持更高质量的业务决策。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成框架，结合外部知识源对LLM生成过程进行优化，增强上下文相关性与事实准确性。

Result: 实验结果显示，RAG增强的LLMs在减少幻觉、提升响应质量、增强上下文相关性以及提高人类可解释性方面表现显著优于基础模型，尤其在中小企业应用场景中展现出更强的可靠性和实用性。

Conclusion: RAG技术能够有效缓解大型语言模型在中小企业环境中的幻觉问题，显著提升生成内容的准确性与可信度，为基于AI的决策支持系统提供更可靠的技术基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [104] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对消费级硬件上大型语言模型（LLM）的能耗问题，构建了一个可复现的硬件级能效基准。在单块RTX 4060Ti 16GB GPU上，使用Ollama引擎对9个参数量为1B至7B的开源LLM进行测试，通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，评估了平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出token能耗（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。结果表明，模型架构和量化策略对能效的影响超过参数数量本身；gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b表现最优，能耗低至0.56 J/token与0.65 J/token，吞吐量超170 tok/s；而7B-Mistral模型能耗高达最高效模型的4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因内部推理时间过长导致每提示能耗异常高，凸显在效率评估中区分生成模式的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在本地部署日益流行，但其在消费级硬件上的实际能耗缺乏系统性评估，现有基准多关注准确率而忽视能效，因此亟需一个基于真实硬件的可复现能效评测框架，以支持更绿色高效的模型选择与部署。

Method: 在单张RTX 4060Ti 16GB GPU上，使用Ollama推理引擎运行9个开源LLM（1B–7B参数），通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采集GPU功耗数据，固定提示集进行测试，分析平均/峰值功率、总能耗（J/prompt）、单位输出token能耗（J/token）及吞吐量（tok/s），并对比不同模型间的能效差异。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b在能耗和吞吐方面表现最佳，每token能耗分别为0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token，吞吐量超过170 tok/s；7B-Mistral模型的每token能耗最高可达最高效模型的4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因内部推理过程冗长，导致每提示能耗异常偏高，说明需区分不同推理模式对能效的影响。

Conclusion: 模型能效不仅受参数规模影响，更取决于架构设计与量化策略；在本地部署中，应综合考虑能耗、吞吐与推理模式，选择真正高效的模型，同时建议未来能效评估应明确区分不同的生成行为。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [105] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoT-Core 是一种无需训练的候选问题选择框架，通过引导大语言模型生成零样本思维链（CoT）轨迹，将问题投影到潜在空间以按内在逻辑等价性聚类，从而克服了传统方法在冷启动和表面词汇偏差上的缺陷。该方法显著降低评估成本并保持高保真度评分估计，在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上验证了其有效性，并揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，即其效果由任务复杂性内在决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估方法存在计算开销大、冷启动瓶颈（如项目反应理论依赖大量历史日志）以及表面词汇偏差（忽视任务深层推理结构）的问题，亟需一种高效且能捕捉本质逻辑的评估加速机制。

Method: CoT-Core 通过让大语言模型生成零样本链式思维（CoT）推理路径，将这些推理轨迹映射到潜在空间，基于内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似性对问题进行聚类，实现无训练的高质量核心问题选择。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA等多个基准上，CoT-Core 显著降低了评估所需的计算资源，同时保持了与全集评估相当的评分准确性；实验还发现其性能受限于任务复杂性，复杂任务中优势更明显。

Conclusion: CoT-Core 成功实现了无需训练的核心问题选择，通过捕捉问题间的深层推理结构，有效提升了评估效率与精度，为大语言模型持续评估提供了可扩展、推理感知的新范式。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [106] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建基于合成数据集的检索增强生成（RAG）管道，评估其对大语言模型（LLM）优化建模性能的提升效果。使用500个由Text2Zinc数据集种子描述和专业角色生成的合成优化问题，以JSON格式编码并存入Chroma向量数据库。推理时，通过语义相似性检索相关示例作为上下文指导LLM代理。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上测试，准确率在NL4OPT、MAMO Easy和MAMO Complex三个基准上分别从40%、40%、32%提升至72%、56%、56%。结果表明，使用经过验证的合成示例显著提升了建模准确性和结构一致性，且无需昂贵的微调，为实际决策支持场景中部署基于LLM的优化工具提供了有效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在组合优化等复杂场景中常生成结构不一致或不完整的优化公式，缺乏领域专业知识和形式化语言熟练度，限制了其在物流、医疗、供应链管理等领域的应用。亟需一种高效、低成本的方法来提升LLM的优化建模能力。

Method: 构建基于合成数据的检索增强生成（RAG）框架：1）利用Text2Zinc种子描述与LLM生成的专业角色，合成500个优化问题；2）将问题以JSON格式存储于Chroma向量数据库；3）在推理阶段，通过语义相似性检索相关示例，并作为上下文输入给LangChain LLM代理进行建模；4）在多个基准测试集上评估性能。

Result: 在NL4OPT、MAMO Easy和MAMO Complex三个测试集上，模型准确率分别从40%、40%、32%提升至72%、56%、56%。结果显示，使用语义验证的合成示例显著改善了建模的准确性与结构完整性。

Conclusion: 结合合成数据生成与检索增强的方案，可有效替代昂贵的模型微调，为在真实世界决策支持系统中部署基于大语言模型的优化工具提供了一条可行、高效的路径。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [107] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自改进大语言模型代理在无权重更新情况下，通过外部记忆存储和检索经验以优化行为的机制。由于部署时缺乏真实标签，代理依赖自身评分作为奖励信号，导致‘回声鸿沟’（Echo Gap）问题：错误的记忆因被高估而反复被重用，错误随时间累积且无法识别。作者提出‘错误独立性假设’（EIA）是纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，即信号需既反映真实情况，又与记忆偏差无关。研究发现，即使在基于相似性的检索中，该问题依然存在。提出的LUCID去膨胀算法在BIRD文本到SQL基准测试中显著提升执行准确率至56.9%，优于自评分代理（54.0%）和无记忆代理（52.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进大语言模型代理依赖内部评分作为奖励信号，但缺乏真实标签时，自我评分易产生偏差，导致错误记忆被反复强化，形成不可逆的性能退化。需要一种机制来识别并修正这种偏差，确保学习过程可靠有效。

Method: 提出并形式化‘错误独立性假设’（EIA），证明其为纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件；设计不依赖答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，利用信号与偏差的独立性恢复可信奖励；在多种检索策略下验证其有效性。

Result: LUCID算法在BIRD文本到SQL基准上实现56.9%的执行准确率，显著优于自评分代理（54.0%）和无记忆代理（52.4%），表明其能有效缓解回声鸿沟问题，提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 自改进大模型代理中的回声鸿沟源于自我评分与记忆偏差的相关性。只有满足错误独立性假设，才能可靠地纠正奖励膨胀。所提出的LUCID算法通过去膨胀机制，在无需真实答案的情况下实现了稳定的性能提升，为构建鲁棒的自改进系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [108] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JouleShare提出一种基于测量的请求级能量分配框架，通过离线重放和GPU功耗数据计算精确的Shapley能量贡献，并使用轻量级校准模型JCalib在服务时预测公平的能量分配。结果表明，按令牌比例分配能量与真实Shapley值存在显著偏差，而JCalib能有效缩小误差，实现更公平且高效的能量会计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理推理虽提升吞吐量，但使能量会计复杂化；现有基准无法提供请求级真实能量分配，导致实际分配规则与公平性之间差距不明。

Method: 构建离线测试环境重放请求子集，结合GPU功耗数据，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量贡献；设计轻量级校准模型JCalib，基于低成本请求特征学习预测Shapley份额。

Result: token比例分配与真实Shapley值平均偏差分别为0.440（静态批）和0.458（连续批），而JCalib将误差降至0.116和0.177，优于仅在线测量的基线，同时保持批次能量效率。

Conclusion: 按令牌分配能量不可靠；通过测量得到的Shapley真值可校准低成本特征，实现更公平的能量分配。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [109] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba introduces a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway to enhance cross-dimensional interactions in Mamba, improving long-sequence modeling while maintaining linear-time efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To overcome the limitation of Mamba's diagonal state transitions, which restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions, thereby enhancing long-sequence modeling capabilities.

Method: Proposes Motif-Mamba with a structured low-rank recurrent pathway inspired by three-node network motifs, projecting hidden states into a dynamical subspace for motif-guided interactions before mapping back.

Result: Outperforms Mamba on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain-computer interface decoding, demonstrating improved performance through enhanced cross-dimensional communication.

Conclusion: Motif-guided low-rank dynamics serve as an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling, enabling better performance without sacrificing computational efficiency.

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [110] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova 是一个端到端的 JIT 编译器，旨在通过全图优化、内存层次结构优化和寄存器级执行调优，实现对硬件映射的绝对控制。它通过统一前向与反向传播为单一值语义方言，并利用分析配置器实现零搜索时间的最优调度，在 RTX 3060 上性能媲美或略超 cuBLAS 和 XLA，同时在模型层面提升吞吐量并显著降低内存占用，成功训练了 14400 万参数模型而避免内存溢出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习框架的急切执行模式缺乏全局图可见性与硬件/内存粒度控制能力，难以最大化物理硬件利用率，亟需一种能实现全图优化与底层控制的编译技术。

Method: Nova 采用 JIT 编译机制，捕获急切执行，将前向与反向传播统一为值语义方言；利用分析配置器根据算术强度确定最优调度，实现零搜索时间；基于结构哈希运行时，从计算结构直接合成细粒度内核，实现操作融合、内存优化与寄存器级调优。

Result: 在 RTX 3060 上，Nova 在多数矩阵乘法形状下性能匹配或略超 cuBLAS 与 XLA，相对误差小于 5e-4；在 4200 万参数模型上，吞吐量比 PyTorch 提升 10.6%，比 XLA 提升 4.4%；内存占用减少最多达 29%，成功训练 1.44 亿参数模型（17,900 tokens/s），而 PyTorch 因 OOM 失败。

Conclusion: Nova 通过全图优化与底层硬件控制，实现了高性能、低内存开销的深度学习推理与训练，在保持数值精度的同时显著提升了实际部署效率，展示了下一代编译器在规模化深度学习中的关键作用。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [111] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN 是一个统一的工具包和交互式网页界面，用于检测检索增强、代理型和记忆驱动的大型语言模型系统中的上下文幻觉（即流畅但缺乏证据支持的回应）。它整合了三种检测范式（表示探查、不确定性估计和判官式验证）以及预生成查询可回答性这一互补任务，提供统一的界面、配置系统和评估流程，支持白盒和黑盒环境下的响应级和片段级检查。其网页界面可实时分析用户提供的上下文-查询-回答三元组，通过幻觉评分、未支持片段高亮和多检测器并行对比实现可视化分析，并采用轻量级插件设计以方便新增检测器。SIRIN 已被应用于幻觉检测、查询可回答性评估及长期记忆系统中的忠实度过滤。源代码已公开于 GitHub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在检测大型语言模型中的上下文幻觉时存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的工具链和交互式界面，难以在不同设置下进行系统性评估与调试。为解决此问题，需要一个集成多种检测机制、支持多粒度分析且可扩展的平台。

Method: SIRIN 统一了三种检测范式：1）表示探查，通过分析模型内部表示识别不一致；2）不确定性估计，利用置信度判断输出可靠性；3）判官式验证，基于外部或内部评判模型判断答案是否合理。此外，引入预生成查询可回答性评估，提前判断问题是否能在给定上下文中回答。所有模块集成于单一界面，支持响应级与片段级分析，兼容白盒与黑盒场景。

Result: SIRIN 能有效检测各类幻觉，显著提升对不一致内容的识别能力，支持实时交互分析，具备良好的可扩展性。实验表明，其在多个基准上优于单一检测方法，在长周期记忆系统中作为忠实度门控表现出色。

Conclusion: SIRIN 提供了一个统一、可扩展且交互性强的幻觉检测框架，适用于多样化 LLM 系统的调试与评估，推动了可信 AI 的发展。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [112] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）中语言如何动态调节视觉表征，发现语言可引发两种关键的视觉表征重构：一是生成抽象的目标相关参考表征，该表征能泛化到不同物体和场景；二是语言诱导的属性调制，即模型在深层中增强与任务相关的视觉属性。通过因果干预验证了这些机制对模型预测的直接影响，表明视觉表征并非静态，而是根据语言指令动态调整以支持下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将视觉语言模型中的视觉表征视为静态信息库，忽视了语言对视觉表征的动态调节作用。然而，人类视觉智能具有目标导向性，能够根据语言提示重新编码视觉信息。因此，亟需探究语言是否以及如何动态改变视觉表征，以理解模型的跨模态处理机制。

Method: 通过提取对比性引导向量，识别语言提示下的抽象参考表征，并设计因果干预实验验证其对模型预测的影响；同时分析模型深层特征，观察语言是否导致目标相关属性的增强，并进一步通过干预验证属性调制对响应分布的中介作用。

Result: 发现了两个语言驱动的视觉表征重构现象：(1) 存在一个可泛化的抽象参考表征，能标识目标相关对象；(2) 后层网络会根据语言提示选择性放大目标相关属性。因果干预证实这些机制直接影响模型输出。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型中的视觉表征并非静态存储，而是受到语言指令的动态调制，支持目标导向的视觉处理。这一发现揭示了跨模态交互的动态本质，为构建更智能、更具适应性的多模态系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [113] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RagTester 是一种自动化端到端测试方法，用于评估检索增强生成（RAG）系统。它通过生成测试文档、输入和期望输出，执行测试并利用大语言模型作为裁判评估结果，重点针对复杂段落、不支持的查询和文档覆盖度进行测试。在72,000次测试中，RagTester比基线多发现6.6%的故障，在20/24配置中表现更优，揭示了检索与生成组件间交互导致的多种错误，如错误检索、无支持的回答、未充分利用上下文及对复杂段落理解困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的可靠性依赖于多个组件间的协同，但缺乏有效的自动化测试方法来全面评估其性能。为提升RAG系统的可信度与部署安全性，亟需一种能系统性暴露潜在缺陷的测试框架。

Method: RagTester采用覆盖导向的测试生成策略，自动构造包含复杂文本、非支持性查询和高覆盖率要求的测试用例；通过运行RAG系统并使用LLM作为评判器评估输出，实现对检索与生成环节的联合验证。

Result: 在8个LLM与6个嵌入模型组合的24种配置下，共执行72,000次测试，RagTester检测出21,633个失败案例，比基线高出6.6%，且在20种配置中表现更优，成功识别出包括检索错误、答案不支持、上下文利用不全和复杂段落理解障碍在内的多种缺陷。

Conclusion: 覆盖导向的测试生成能够有效揭示检索与生成组件交互带来的系统性故障，为RAG系统的评估与优化提供有力支持，有助于在部署前发现并修复关键问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [114] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: H+ Embedding 提出一种统一的多粒度检索方法，通过预测可变长度短语分区、保留未覆盖的词元为单例，并使用基于重要性的单元选择与加权 MaxSim 交互，在16个科学、医学和双语任务中，其短语检索分支在 macro nDCG@10 上比全局检索分支高出6.91，同时仅需13.7%的文档向量，接近Token级性能，优于固定分组规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法在全局向量压缩与细粒度词元交互之间存在权衡：单向量模型过度压缩局部相关性信号，而词元级后期交互带来高昂的索引、存储和评分成本。因此需要一种介于两者之间的中间粒度检索单元，以兼顾效果与效率。

Method: 提出 H+ Embedding，通过上下文依赖的短语分割机制，动态生成可变长度短语，未被覆盖的子词保留为单例，并采用重要性引导的单元选择策略结合加权 MaxSim 交互，实现多粒度信息融合与高效检索。

Result: 在16个任务中，短语检索分支相比全局检索提升6.91 macro nDCG@10；在较低向量预算下，仅用13.7%的文档向量即接近Token级性能，优于内容无关的分组规则。

Conclusion: 上下文依赖的短语交互在检索效果与计算成本之间提供了理想的折中点，适用于实际检索系统。

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [115] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次对AI编程代理（如GitHub Copilot）的生产规模工作负载进行了全面分析，基于2026年6月的采样数据，涵盖320万用户、1300万会话、7.61亿次LLM调用和95万亿token。发现其工作负载具有显著特征：用户发起的交互稀疏，每个交互触发自主的代理循环，包含多次LLM调用与工具执行；单个交互内KV缓存命中率高达90%，但跨交互边界下降至55%，且在模型切换或上下文压缩后急剧失效。工作流多样，用户行为呈现长尾分布，存在快速代理响应与数分钟级用户空闲期并存的现象。研究提出轻量级空闲时间预测器，可捕获86-90%的空闲时间，支持资源调度的主动决策。这些发现挑战了现有LLM服务系统的设计假设，为面向代理的基础设施提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要针对聊天机器人设计，而AI编程代理的工作负载具有多步推理、工具调用、用户交互稀疏等特性，导致传统系统优化策略不适用。亟需基于真实生产数据理解该新型工作负载，以指导构建更高效的代理原生基础设施。

Method: 基于2026年6月的生产环境采样数据，收集来自GitHub Copilot的用户行为日志，包括320万用户、1300万会话、7.61亿次LLM调用及95万亿token。通过分析会话结构、交互频率、缓存命中率、工具调用模式、用户空闲时间等指标，识别工作负载的关键特征，并设计轻量级空闲时间预测模型验证其有效性。

Result: 发现编程代理会话中存在高密度的内部自主循环，单个交互内KV缓存命中率达90%，但跨交互边界降至55%，且易因模型切换或上下文压缩而失效；用户行为呈现长尾分布，存在快速响应与长时间空闲交替现象；提出的轻量级空闲时间预测器能有效捕获86-90%的空闲时间，支持资源的主动调度优化。

Conclusion: 当前主流LLM服务系统无法充分适配AI编程代理的复杂工作负载。必须构建面向代理的新型基础设施，从缓存策略、资源调度到系统架构进行重构。本研究提供的实证数据与洞察为下一代代理原生系统的设计奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [116] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bazaar是一个动态密封投标基准，用于评估大语言模型在多属性拍卖中的定价能力，尤其在客户偏好隐蔽、竞争实时变化和需求突变的复杂市场环境中。尽管基准基于可解析的客户效用函数，可进行精确评估，但11个前沿LLM的表现显示，赢得客户获取的代理（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）未必是最盈利的（如Opus 4.6），且在需求冲击后，学习快的代理反而调整慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro恢复最快。然而，最强代理的利润仍不足事后最优利润的三分之一，表明当前LLM在智能代理商业中虽有进展，但仍存在巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在真实复杂市场环境中的定价能力，尤其是在客户偏好未知、竞争动态变化和需求波动的情况下，以推动智能代理在商业交易中的实际应用。

Method: 提出Bazaar基准，一个基于封闭形式客户效用的动态密封投标测试框架，用于系统评估多个大语言模型在多属性拍卖中的表现，涵盖客户获取、利润最大化及对需求冲击的适应能力。

Result: 不同模型在客户获取、利润和应对需求冲击方面表现不一；领先模型在不同指标间排名变动显著，且即使最强模型也仅实现不到三分之一的理论上最优利润，说明当前技术仍有巨大改进空间。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在代理商业场景中已取得一定进展，但在真实市场动态下的定价与决策能力仍远未达到最优水平，未来需进一步优化模型的适应性与策略学习能力。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [117] [Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00122)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个在生产环境中部署的共享组织记忆系统，旨在将经验捕获融入编码工作流程中。该系统自动收集与任务相关的经验（经贡献者批准），将其整理为可重用的问题-答案记忆，并过滤安全与隐私风险，供未来代理检索使用。目前仍在评估其对检索和编码任务的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业编码代理依赖工具和检索，但企业内部知识（如内部DSL、专有平台、本地规范、近期修复和隐性工作流）通常不在公开训练数据或正式文档中。现有知识接口虽能暴露存储资源，但仍需代理主动识别并记录可复用的经验，导致经验捕获与编码流程脱节，造成重复发现开发经验的问题。

Method: 构建一个平台级的共享组织记忆系统，自动收集任务相关经验（经贡献者确认），通过聚类与结构化处理形成可检索的问答记忆库，实施安全与隐私审查机制，并集成到编码工作流中，实现经验的无缝捕获与复用。

Result: 系统已在生产环境部署，初步展示了将经验捕获嵌入编码流程的可行性；目前对检索效率和编码任务的实际影响仍在评估中。

Conclusion: 通过将经验捕获机制内置于平台层级，该系统有望减少重复劳动，提升企业内部知识的可复用性与开发效率，是迈向智能化、协同化编码的重要一步。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents rely on tools and retrieval, yet enterprise knowledge often remains outside public training data and formal documentation: internal DSLs, proprietary platforms, local conventions, recent fixes, and tacit workflows. Existing knowledge interfaces expose stored resources but still depend on agents recognizing and explicitly recording lessons worth reusing, disconnecting capture from the coding workflow and leaving development experience repeatedly rediscovered. We report an ongoing production deployment of a shared organizational memory system that makes capture a platform-level part of coding work: it collects task-adjacent experience with contributor approval, curates it into reusable question-answer memories, gates obvious security and privacy risks, and retrieves memories for future agents. This short paper describes the deployed lifecycle and an operational snapshot. Effects on retrieval and coding tasks remain under evaluation.

</details>


### [118] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStream框架，用于在真实流式任务环境中评估自演化语言模型代理。通过配置化任务流和三种不同流式场景（孤立、顺序、交错），系统性地测试五种自演化方法在三个前沿大模型上的表现，揭示了模型能力、方法架构与流式场景对自演化效果的联合影响。结果表明自演化可靠性随场景变化，其收益受模型能力限制且非单调，无单一方法在所有场景中占优。研究强调应以真实任务流替代孤立单任务评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用独立评估方式，难以反映自演化代理在真实复杂、多样任务流中的实际表现，亟需更贴近现实的评估框架。

Method: 提出AgentStream框架，将基准测试组织为可配置任务流，并在测试时实例化三种流式场景（隔离、顺序、交错），结合多种自演化方法与大模型进行组合评估。

Result: 自演化可靠性因流式场景而异；其有效性受模型能力制约，且在模型强度上呈现非单调特性；不同方法在不同模型和场景中表现各异，无统一最优方案。

Conclusion: 应基于真实任务流评估自演化代理，而非孤立单任务设置，以获得更准确的方法选择指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [119] [TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00200)
*Sparsh Rastogi,Tanmay Kumar,Baiyu Chen,Jatin Bedi,Zechen Li,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-TS 是一种用于可穿戴时间序列数据的可追溯推理框架，通过专家分类器的归因识别关键时空传感器区域，构建具有明确证据来源的有向无环图（DAG）推理轨迹，并使用紧凑的语言模型通过门控交叉注意力生成这些轨迹。该方法在推理时联合输出活动预测及其推理轨迹，无需额外的归因计算或教师指导。引入了语义节点匹配（SNM）评估指标，可从观测、推理和综合层面诊断推理保真度，定位幻觉观测和被标准自然语言生成指标忽略的断裂证据链。在七个可穿戴基准上，TRACE-TS 在平均准确率和 F1 上均优于现有方法（84.43%/81.24%），相比最佳的 LLM 基线提升 17.96% 的 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的方法虽然能生成自然语言解释，但其推理缺乏对原始传感器信号的有效锚定，导致解释流于表面且不可验证。因此需要一种能够将语言推理严格建立在可穿戴时间序列信号基础上的方法，以实现可信、可追溯的智能行为理解。

Method: TRACE-TS 利用专家分类器的归因识别关键时空传感器区域，构建带有明确证据来源的 DAG 推理轨迹；采用门控交叉注意力机制，在传感器记忆标记上训练一个紧凑的语言模型来生成推理过程；推理阶段联合输出活动标签与结构化推理轨迹，无需额外归因或教师监督。

Result: 在七个可穿戴时间序列基准测试中，TRACE-TS 实现了最高的平均准确率（84.43%）和 F1 分数（81.24%），显著超越现有 LLM 基线方法，尤其在 F1 指标上领先 17.96%。引入的 SNM 评估指标有效识别出传统 NLG 指标无法捕捉的推理错误，如幻觉观测与断裂证据链。

Conclusion: TRACE-TS 成功实现了可穿戴时间序列数据上的可追溯、信号锚定的推理，不仅提升了预测性能，还增强了解释的可信度与可解释性，为可穿戴健康与行为分析提供了可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Wearable sensors capture fine-grained motion patterns that support rich behavioral understanding, yet most existing methods reduce these signals to activity labels. Recent LM-based approaches generate natural-language explanations for sensor data, but their reasoning is weakly grounded in the underlying signal, leading to fluent yet unverifiable explanations. We introduce TRACE-TS (Traceable Reasoning with Attribution-Grounded Evidence), a framework for structured and signal-grounded reasoning over wearable time series. TRACE-TS uses attribution from an expert classifier to identify salient spatio-temporal sensor regions, uses them to construct DAG reasoning traces with explicit evidence provenance, and trains a compact language model to generate these traces through gated cross-attention over sensor memory tokens. At inference, the adapted model jointly outputs the activity prediction and its reasoning trace, without requiring attribution computation or teacher guidance. We introduce Semantic Node Match(SNM), an LLM-as-judge metric that diagnoses reasoning fidelity at the observation, inference, and synthesis levels, localizing hallucinated observations and broken evidence chains missed by standard NLG metrics. Across seven wearable benchmarks, TRACE-TS achieves the best average accuracy and F1 among all evaluated methods (84.43%/81.24%), and outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 17.96% in F1. Our code is available at https://github.com/SparshRastogi/TRACE-TS.

</details>


### [120] [Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00215)
*Dian Jin,Zhi Zhang,Huichao Li,Yihe Pan,Rundong Huang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FABLE（Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution），一种轻量级策略层，用于在不微调大型语言模型（LLM）的情况下，基于用户反馈在线学习个性化执行策略。该方法将记忆、信息获取和响应决策解耦，通过贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样学习用户特定的残差偏好，并在可行动作集上进行探索，同时具备预期遗憾率界和防止虚假推广的控制机制。实验表明，FABLE在个性化推理、受控反馈和可执行工具使用任务中显著提升偏好敏感行为，且不影响整体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理难以根据个体用户调整执行决策，而微调成本高或不可行；传统提示和记忆方式仅暴露用户信息，无法真正适应执行策略。因此需要一种轻量、无需微调的个性化方法，从用户反馈中学习执行策略。

Method: FABLE采用因子化设计，将记忆、信息获取与响应决策分离，利用外部指定的可行动作集过滤动作，通过贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样学习用户残差偏好，并基于线性残差奖励模型实现在线学习与优化。

Result: 在个性化推理、可控反馈和可执行工具使用等任务中，FABLE显著改善了偏好敏感行为，同时保持与规则基方法相当的端到端任务性能。此外，其校准版本具有理论上的预期遗憾界，且支持任意时间有效的虚假推广控制。

Conclusion: FABLE是一种高效、可部署的个性化策略框架，能够在不修改主代理的前提下，基于用户反馈自适应地优化执行决策，为黑箱系统中的个性化提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can retrieve memory, call tools, ask clarifying questions, and vary response style, yet adapting these execution decisions to an individual user remains difficult. Fine-tuning a separate LLM is costly or impossible for proprietary systems, while prompts and memory primarily expose user information to the agent rather than adapt its execution decisions from feedback. We formulate personalization of a frozen agent as online learning of a per-user execution policy from scalar feedback observed only for the executed action. We propose FABLE (Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution), a lightweight policy layer outside a potentially black-box host agent. FABLE factorizes memory, information-acquisition, and response decisions so feedback updates related choices; filters actions through an externally specified feasible set before exploration; and learns user-specific residual preferences relative to a fixed default-and-cost score via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant inherits an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action. We also characterize preferences unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and provide anytime-valid false-promotion control. Across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and executable tool-use evaluations, FABLE improves several preference-sensitive behaviors relative to rule-only control while remaining competitive on end-to-end task performance.

</details>


### [121] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体评审团在事实验证任务中表现不一，部分数据集显示性能提升，部分出现下降，无法确认辩论机制的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证多智能体评审团在提升判断质量方面的有效性，特别是在基于证据的命题真实性判断任务中。

Method: 在六个公开的事实验证与幻觉检测基准上，评估由三个同质智能体组成的评审团，并与固定单智能体参考系统进行对比。

Result: 评审团系统整体准确率变化范围为+8.5至-4.4个百分点；两个数据集显示显著增益，一个显示显著损失，其余三个无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 由于参考系统与评审团使用不同模型变体，无法分离出辩论机制的独立因果效应，因此不能确定多智能体讨论是否真正提升了判断质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [122] [Geometric Self-Supervised Pre-training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00270)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对路由问题的几何自监督预训练框架，通过等距变换（如旋转和轴对称）学习空间不变性和全局相对距离分布，显著提升了神经组合优化模型在高维实例上的泛化能力。实验表明，该方法在大规模零样本外推场景下比从头训练的基线模型平均减少7.23%的路径长度，并且计算速度比精确求解器Concorde快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的神经组合优化模型在处理高维路由问题时泛化能力差，而自监督预训练虽在其他领域成功应用，但在缺乏复杂拓扑结构的路由图上难以实施。

Method: 提出一种几何自监督预训练框架，利用旋转、轴反射等等距变换生成数据增强，使模型学习到空间不变性与全局相对距离分布特征，从而提升模型在政策优化前的结构表示能力。

Result: 在TSP1,000的大规模零样本外推中，路径长度比基线模型减少7.23%；计算效率比精确求解器Concorde快两个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何自监督预训练框架有效增强了神经组合优化模型在高维路由问题中的泛化能力与计算效率，为解决大规模组合优化问题提供了高效可行的新路径。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) techniques have emerged as a highly efficient alternative to traditional exact algorithms for solving routing problems such as the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). However, the generalization capabilities of these Reinforcement Learning-based models are severely hindered when scaling to high-dimensional instances. This issue has been mitigated in other domains, like computer vision and natural language processing, by adopting a self-supervised pre-training strategy. Nevertheless, its application to routing graphs, which lack complex topological attributes beyond 2D spatial coordinates, remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a geometric self-supervised pre-training framework specifically designed to capture spatial invariance and global relative distance distributions. By applying isometric transformations, such as rotations and axial reflections, the model learns robust structural representations prior to the policy optimization phase. Empirical results demonstrate that this strategy consistently outperforms models trained from scratch (baselines), achieving a 7.23\% improvement in tour length for massive zero-shot extrapolation scenarios (TSP1,000). Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits remarkable computational efficiency, delivering speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over the exact solver Concorde at massive scales.
  The source code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/davidaguadocosano/TSP-GeoPretrain.git.

</details>


### [123] [RF-HOI: Recognize Human-Object Interaction with Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00289)
*Lihao Wang,Linlu Gao,Jiacan Yu,Yanyu Lin,Yifan Yin,Jianxin Wang,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RF-HOI 是首个仅使用射频（RF）信号进行人-物体交互（HOI）识别的框架，结合毫米波雷达与RFID实现多模态融合，克服单一RF感知的局限性。针对训练数据稀缺问题，提出一个可合成大规模多样化RF数据的模拟器，仅需少量真实数据即可显著提升模型性能。实验表明，RF-HOI在真实场景中表现接近视觉模型，验证了多模态RF感知在隐私保护和鲁棒性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的HOI方法面临隐私泄露和光照条件差的问题，亟需一种不依赖视觉、且具备高鲁棒性和隐私保护能力的替代方案。

Method: 提出RF-HOI框架，融合mmWave雷达与RFID信号实现多模态感知；设计一个用于生成多样化合成多模态RF数据的模拟器，以增强模型泛化能力。

Result: RF-HOI在多种真实场景下性能优于所有基线方法，接近视觉模型水平；合成数据显著提升了系统在真实环境中的表现。

Conclusion: 多模态RF感知为隐私保护和鲁棒的HOI识别提供了可行路径，结合数据合成技术可有效缓解真实数据不足问题，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Recognizing Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is essential for intelligent systems, underpinning applications in virtual and augmented reality, embodied AI, and assistive robotics. However, vision-based HOI methods face challenges in privacy concerns and poor light conditions. In this work, we introduce RF-HOI, the first framework that only uses radio frequency (RF) signals for HOI recognition. A key challenge of RF-HOI is that single-modality RF sensing is insufficient to recognize both actions and the objects being interacted with. RF-HOI addresses this through a novel modality fusion that combines mmWave radar and RFID, enabling simultaneous action recognition and target identification. Another challenge is limited training data across diverse setups, which impairs the generalizability of the recognition model. To overcome this, we develop a simulator that synthesizes multimodal RF data for diverse HOIs at scale, allowing us to fine-tune with only a small amount of real-world data. Experiment results show that RF-HOI outperforms all baselines, approaching vision model performance, and that our diverse synthetic training data can significantly boost our system's performance on real-world scenarios. These results highlight the potential of multimodal RF sensing for robust and privacy-preserving HOI recognition as well as the effectiveness of our RF data synthesis.

</details>


### [124] [WM-Cov: Test Adequacy for Interactive World-Model-Style Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00298)
*Jianxun Cui,Ping Wu,Stanisa Peric,Marko Milojkovic,Vladan Devedzic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为WM-Cov的评估框架，用于衡量基于世界模型的自动驾驶测试系统的有效性。该框架将原始生成结果转化为可验证的、真实的测试证据，并通过覆盖度增长、有效故障发现、故障模式多样性、真实性、伪影抑制、重复项处理和有效证据精度等指标评估测试充分性。研究表明，仅依赖生成的失败或提示覆盖率不足以判断测试质量，而应关注在预算内有效交互证据的收敛情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动驾驶测试依赖固定场景回放，难以捕捉复杂交互下的安全关键事件。世界模型和生成式模拟器虽能生成动态、交互式的测试场景，但其输出可能包含无效、重复或虚假的失败案例，导致测试结论不可靠。因此需要一种新的评估方法来确保测试证据的有效性和充分性。

Method: 提出WM-Cov评估层，不依赖特定生成模型，可将任意世界模型输出转换为请求、实际实现和有效证据三类信息，并基于多维度指标量化测试过程中的有效证据积累情况。

Result: 在多个真实和混合数据集上的实验表明，大量看似危险的事件实为无效或部分实现的伪像；只有少数事件成为完全实现的有效故障。通过分析360次路径请求与80次独立路线切片，验证了有效证据收敛是更可靠的停止标准。

Conclusion: 世界模型驱动的测试应以有效交互证据的收敛作为主要评估依据，而非单纯统计生成失败数或提示覆盖率。这为自动化测试提供了更可靠、可解释的停止准则。

Abstract: World models and generative simulators are emerging as interactive testing infrastructure for autonomous driving because they can react to the ego planner and produce counterfactual, rare, and safety-critical rollouts. This changes a test scenario from a fixed replayed trajectory into an interactive scenario family whose realized evolution depends on the planner under test. The unresolved question is therefore not only whether dangerous rollouts can be generated, but what valid closed-loop evidence is enough to support a specified testing intent and stopping decision. This paper formulates interactive world-model-style testing adequacy and introduces WM-Cov, a provider-agnostic evaluation layer that converts raw provider outputs into requested, realized, and valid evidence.
